RESERVE for a failing or too-expensive panel model: Moonshot Kimi-K2 class, then MiniMax. PANEL CACHE: key every panel response by (sentence_id, model_id, prompt_sha1, sorted class_ids), and never change keys for unchanged items. The sibling run re-billed everything after a key change.\\n\\n=== STEP 1. SENTENCE SELECTION (0:35-1:05), deterministic, seed 0, sha1 ordering ===\\n1a. Screen exclusion set: normalised-text hashes, where norm = lowercase, curly quotes to straight, strip everything except [a-z0-9 ], collapse whitespace. Cover all premises and conclusions of tasksource/folio validation (download via aii-hf-datasets), local folio_refined_validation.csv, the curated FOLIO and MALLS jsonl, MALLS-v0.1-test, and the Logic-LM FOLIO_dev contexts (step 6a). Log the size of the exclusion set.\\n1b. MALLS-train pool: 27,284 items. Parse gold with the fixed fol.py and drop unparseable gold (expect ≈334; log it). Dedupe on norm text. Drop excluded hashes. Compute words = len(text.split()); n_conditions (complexity_counts.nconds on gold); n_quant; depth; text_conditions = count of \\\\b(if|when|whenever|who|that|which|whose|unless|provided|only if|as long as|in case)\\\\b; exception_type. exception_type priority: unless > except > excluding > other_than > without > but_not. 'but not' counts only if the text does NOT match 'but not both' or 'either .* but not'.\\n1c. Stratum assignment, disjoint, in priority order. EXC = 100: all parseable items with unless/except/excluding/other than (≈72 expected), filled with 'without' then non-XOR 'but not' items with ≥15 words, sha1-ordered. L25 = 200 items with ≥25 words and n_conditions ≥3, sampled evenly over word bins 25-29 / 30-34 / ≥35 (take the shortfall from neighbouring bins). L20 = 150 items with 20-24 words and n_conditions ≥3. Supply check against complexity_counts.out.txt: 1,501 L25 and 4,539 ≥20-word items exist, so shortfalls should not occur; log them if they do.\\n1d. CTRL = 150 FOLIO-train premises. Download tasksource/folio train (premises-FOL is newline-separated). Match stories to local yfxiao__folio-refined__train.csv by the normalised premise list, then pair premises line by line; skip stories whose line counts differ. For each pair (orig_fol, refined_fol), set agreement_type in {IDENTICAL_STRING, EQ, VOCAB, GRAN, NONEQ, UNPARSEABLE} using equivalent_modulo_vocab. The eligible pool is EQ/VOCAB/GRAN/IDENTICAL_STRING. Prefer non-identical agreement: an identical string is only weak evidence that the refiner endorsed it, not independent annotation. Record agreement_type, and set reference = the refined formula. Stratify 50 items under 12 words, 60 at 12-19 and 40 at ≥20 words (take all ≥20-word items if fewer exist). Before sampling, reserve 6 FEW-SHOT EXEMPLARS from this pool and exclude them from CTRL. They must cover a universal conditional, a relative-clause restrictor, either-or (⊕), a negation, a named individual and a ≥2-condition conditional.\\n1e. Write sentences.json (600 rows with sentence_id = sha1('heldout|'+norm)[:12]) and prereg_strata.json: strata definitions, counts, exclusion counts and the testability rule, timestamped and frozen here.\\n\\n=== STEP 2. CANDIDATE GENERATION (1:05-1:50; sub-cap $3.5) ===\\n2a. Prompt (prompts/fewshot_v1.txt; save its sha1). The system message states the syntax: ∀ ∃ ¬ ∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕, CamelCase predicates with explicit arguments, lowercase constants, one formula, output exactly 'FOL: <formula>'. It asks for no ontology or vocabulary, which matches the user's ungrounded setting, and says nothing about how to render 'unless' (no convention hint). Then the 6 exemplars, then the sentence. The zero-shot variant (prompts/zeroshot_v1.txt) is the same text without exemplars. Settings: temperature 0, max_tokens 600, reasoning disabled or minimal where supported, 1 retry on transport error only. Do not re-sample when the output does not parse: an unparseable output IS the data.\\n2b. PILOT: 10 sentences × every slot. Measure the parse rate and the actual $/call from usage. Extrapolate the cost to the full sweep and log it. If the projection is above $3.5, first cut the frontier subset to 120, then cut zero-shot to 1 family. Drop any slot with parse rate <30% on the pilot and replace it with the reserve generator.\\n2c. Full sweep, asyncio with Semaphore(16), and resumable: responses are keyed by (sentence_id, slot, prompt_variant) and appended to raw/generations.jsonl. The runs: 600 × 10 slots (G1, G1b, G2-G9) few-shot, 600 × 2 zero-shot (G1b, G2), and 200 × F. The frontier subset is 60 L25 + 50 L20 + 40 EXC + 50 CTRL, sha1-ordered. Expected ≈7,400 calls, ≈$2.5-3.3.\\n2d. ccg2lambda. Load HF kenken6696/folio_by_ccg2lambda (train 1,640 / validation 365; columns original, logical_form; NLTK-style event semantics such as 'exists x.', '_larry(x1)', Subj/Acc roles). Use ONLY the train split and match on norm(original) to the CTRL sentences. Convert each match with nltk.sem.logic.Expression.fromstring, then emit fol.py syntax (strip the leading '_' and CamelCase the names; event variables stay). Label with system='ccg2lambda' and system_class='symbolic_eventsem'. Expect the aligner to fail on almost all of them (neo-Davidsonian events), so their labels come from the panel (tier B). Report the overlap count. If it is below 30 sentences, still include them but note that they are not analysable per system.\\n2e. Add the MALLS GPT-4 gold itself as system='malls_gpt4_gold' on the 450 MALLS sentences. It is a real 2023 GPT-4 output, and its label comes from the blind gold audit (label_source='panel_only'). Mark it so that iteration 2 can include or exclude it.\\n\\n=== STEP 3. AUTO-LABEL PASS 1 AND CLASS COLLAPSE (1:50-2:40, runs in the background while Step 4 runs) ===\\n3a. Parse and normalise every candidate. Per sentence, union-find all parseable formulas (candidates + reference) into EQUIVALENCE CLASSES modulo vocabulary: compare each new formula with the existing class representatives using equivalent_modulo_vocab; an UNKNOWN result opens its own class. Use a ProcessPool with 4 workers and a wall-clock guard. Scale 20 sentences → 100 → all, and extrapolate time at each step. Expected: at most 60 checks per sentence, about 20-30 min total.\\n3b. For every class not equivalent to the reference, run minimal_typed_repair(rep, reference) with budget_s=8. Labels propagate from the class representative to all member rows; store class_id and class_size. If the projected repair time exceeds 75 min, lower budget_s to 5 and log it (COMPOUND_TIMEOUT rises; the panel covers these).\\n\\n=== STEP 4. PANEL CALIBRATION GATE (overlaps Step 3; sub-cap $0.3 + $0.4 for the track-H real-error check) ===\\n4a. SYNTHETIC KNOWN-LABEL SET, 80 items on 20 sentences. Sentences: the 20 CTRL-pool premises NOT used in CTRL or exemplars, TRUSTED_AGREED, 10 of them with ≥15 words. Per sentence, build 4 variants (shuffled, sha1 seed): 2 UNFAITHFUL typed perturbations of the reference and 2 FAITHFUL variants. Perturbations: NEG, REV, QUANT, DROP of a condition, ADD of an atom from another sentence, SWAP; balanced 20 at DOWN positions (antecedent/restrictor) and 20 at UP positions, with the position computed by z3 monotonicity (fol.profile). Every perturbation must be z3-verified NON-equivalent. Faithful variants: 20 STRICT rewrites (conjunct reorder, De Morgan, contrapositive, prenex, variable rename), each z3-verified equivalent, and 20 synonym renames of predicate names (known VOCAB). Record the variant type.\\n4b. Run each panel model (plus Sonnet-class as a P1 alternative) in EXACTLY the production format (Step 5 prompt, disguised, batched per sentence). Gate per model: balanced accuracy ≥0.80 AND recall ≥0.70 on the faithful class AND ≥0.70 on the unfaithful class; report the DOWN vs UP split. A model below the gate is replaced by a reserve-family model, which is re-gated. If fewer than 3 models pass, run with 2 and mark every 1-1 split CONTESTED.\\n4c. REAL-ERROR CHECK against human experts: run the same panel on the curated track-H pairs (Step 6c; the 99 non-equivalent original→corrected pairs), with both formulas shown blind per sentence. Human verdict: corrected = faithful, original = unfaithful, excluding ambiguity-flagged items (READING_CHOICE, which are reported separately). Report per-model and majority accuracy on the unambiguous pairs, split by auto census class (single-op / two-op / COMPOUND). This is the panel's accuracy on REAL errors, the number the synthetic gate cannot give. It must be reported next to every panel-decided label rate. Contamination caveat: the corrected gold has been public since 2026-06; the disguise mitigates this.\\n\\n=== STEP 5. BLIND PANEL PASS: GOLD AUDIT + ADJUDICATION IN ONE (2:40-3:30; sub-cap $3.0) ===\\n5a. Disguise (labeller/disguise.py). Build a per-sentence lexicon: content-word stems of the text (spaCy en_core_web_sm lemmas + Porter) ∪ CamelCase/underscore tokens of every predicate and constant across ALL formulas of that sentence. Map each stem to a pronounceable CVCVC nonce, seeded by sha1(sentence_id), rejected if in WordNet, bijective. Rewrite the text token-wise, and rename predicate and constant names token-wise; logic, function, quantifier and number words stay unchanged. Guards: every disguised formula must parse; no two distinct symbols may collapse to one name (append a suffix if they would); on 50 sentences, assert that the pairwise equivalence structure is unchanged after disguise. The disguised text and formulas become row fields.\\n5b. ONE call per sentence per panel model. Shown: the disguised sentence and a shuffled list of the distinct disguised class representatives, with the reference class hidden among them, unlabelled, and UNPARSEABLE outputs not shown. Cap at 8 classes per call and split into 2 calls above that. Instruction: for each formula, decide whether it is faithful to the sentence, i.e. it says what the sentence says, no more and no less; different predicate names, decomposition, or logically equivalent form are fine. If unfaithful, give ALL applicable census operators from {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, MOVE, DROP, ADD, SWAP, BIND, SCOPE, UNGLUE, OTHER}, a confidence 1-5, and flag whether the sentence is AMBIGUOUS (two legitimate readings). Output strict JSON; on a JSON failure, one re-ask. Settings: temperature 0, reasoning off or low, and reasoning tokens counted in cost.\\n5c. Cost estimate before launch: ≈600 calls × 3 models at about 1.1k input / 0.3k output tokens, i.e. about $0.004 per sentence for the three models together, ≈$2.4. Run 30 sentences first and re-extrapolate. If the projection is above $3.0, adjudicate CTRL only for classes auto-labelled VOCAB_GRAN/COMPOUND/TIMEOUT plus a 20% sentence sample (the direction's original scheme), and keep full coverage for the 450 MALLS sentences.\\n5d. GOLD AUDIT from the same votes, per sentence, using the reference class's votes. ≥2/3 faithful → GOLD_PANEL_OK (MALLS) or TRUSTED_AGREED (CTRL). ≥2/3 unfaithful → GOLD_WRONG (MALLS) or DISPUTED_REFERENCE (CTRL; the row moves to tier C).\\n\\n=== STEP 5e. REFERENCE REPAIR FOR GOLD_WRONG (sub-cap $0.6) ===\\nEach panel model writes its own FOL for the ORIGINAL sentence with the Step 2 few-shot prompt. If ≥2 of the 3 are mutually equivalent_modulo_vocab (EQ/VOCAB/GRAN), the reference becomes the one with the smallest symbol count among the agreeing formulas, status PANEL_REPAIRED. Otherwise the status is NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE. Then re-run Step 3b auto-labelling for those sentences against the new reference; the panel votes on candidate classes are reference-free and stay valid. Rows for NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE sentences get auto_label='NO_REF' and a panel-only label (tier C).\\n\\n=== STEP 5f. FINAL LABEL RULE (write it in the card verbatim) ===\\n- auto CORRECT (plain z3 EQ to the reference) → final CORRECT, tier A. Panel dissent is logged as panel false-alarm evidence.\\n- auto ERROR(ops) → final ERROR(ops), tier A, unless ≥2/3 panel models say faithful → CONTESTED (a likely convention, aligner or reference issue; convention_flags are recorded).\\n- auto VOCAB_GRAN → panel majority: faithful → CORRECT (correct_not_equivalent=true, tier B); unfaithful → ERROR('MEANING_RENAME' + panel ops, tier B).\\n- auto COMPOUND or TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN → panel majority: unfaithful → ERROR('COMPOUND', panel ops, tier B); faithful → CORRECT (correct_not_equivalent=true, tier B).\\n- If the panel majority flags the sentence AMBIGUOUS, add reading_choice=true and keep it out of primary pooling, as a separate class.\\n- A missing vote (a model failed) with a 1-1 split → CONTESTED.\\n- UNPARSEABLE → final UNPARSEABLE, kept in the denominator.\\n- NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE or DISPUTED_REFERENCE → final = panel majority, tier C (secondary only).\\nPrimary analyses use tiers A+B, exclude CONTESTED, and report sensitivity with CONTESTED counted as CORRECT and as ERROR.\\n\\n=== STEP 6. SCREEN AUDIT (3:30-4:15; sub-cap $1.3) ===\\n6a. Rebuild the screen exactly as the strategy defines it. Fetch the Logic-LM outputs from https://raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json and parse the 'FOL ::: NL' lines of raw_logic_programs. TRACK L: a (system, sentence, FOL) triple whose norm(sentence) matches a curated FOLIO-validation conclusion (reference = corrected gold) or a FOLIO-validation premise where original gold (tasksource validation) ≡ folio_refined_validation modulo vocabulary (reference = the refined formula). Match-rate fallback: if fewer than 150 conclusions match, add the matched stories' agreeing premises, as in the strategy. TRACK H: the 302 curated items, candidate = original gold, reference = corrected gold; ambiguity-flagged items → READING_CHOICE. item_id = sha1(system+'|'+normtext+'|'+candidate_fol)[:16], with candidate_fol the RAW string as it appears in the source. Also store (system, raw_text, normtext, raw_fol) so that iteration 2 can re-join if another experiment normalised differently. Report the match rate.\\n6b. Auto-label with the same labeller. Panel scope (sentence-level, direction's scheme): every sentence having any VOCAB_GRAN/COMPOUND/TIMEOUT class, plus a 20% sha1 random sample of the remaining sentences, plus all 99 track-H non-equivalent pairs (already run in 4c; reuse them, do not re-call). Same disguise, prompt and combination rule. Emit screen_adjudicated_labels.json as {item_id: {track, system, auto_label, repair_ops, panel_votes, final_label, label_tier, reading_choice, join_keys}}. Rows not sampled get final_label = auto_label and label_tier = 'auto_only'.\\n\\n=== STEP 7. ASSEMBLE, VALIDATE, DOCUMENT (4:15-5:30) ===\\n7a. data_out.json with 4 datasets. FIRST read the aii-json exp_sel_data_out schema. If input/output must be strings, input = json.dumps({text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, system, prompt_variant}, ensure_ascii=False) and output = final_label, with every other field mirrored as metadata_* keys: metadata_fold ('heldout' | 'screen_audit' | 'calibration'), auto_label, repair_ops, repair_status, panel_votes (per model: faithful, ops, conf, ambiguous), label_tier, label_source, correct_not_equivalent, reading_choice, gold_audit_flag, reference_status, convention_flags, strata {words, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, text_conditions, exception_type, source_stratum}, disguised_text, disguised_fol, class_id, class_size, sentence_id, item_id, normalisation_applied, generator_model_id, cost_usd. heldout item_id = sha1(system|normtext|raw_fol)[:16] (same recipe as the screen). Validate with aii-json, make mini and preview variants, and split files with aii-file-size-limit if needed.\\n7b. dataset_card.md, reporting DATA-QUALITY statistics only, no metric results:\\n- ERROR and CORRECT counts, rows and distinct sentences, per stratum (L25, L20, EXC by exception_type, CTRL by length bin) × tier; the testability declaration per stratum, copied into prereg_strata.json with a timestamp;\\n- gold-error rate of MALLS-train items per stratum (GOLD_WRONG / audited) with a Wilson 95% CI, and PANEL_REPAIRED vs NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE counts;\\n- correct-but-not-equivalent rate (final CORRECT among rows not plain-EQ to the reference), per stratum and per system;\\n- the automatic labeller's precision and recall vs the panel majority (binary auto CORRECT/VOCAB_GRAN vs ERROR/COMPOUND, on all panel-covered classes), plus the confusion matrix by auto class;\\n- panel calibration table (synthetic gate per model, DOWN/UP, STRICT/rename), real-error accuracy on track H, Fleiss κ per stratum, CONTESTED rate;\\n- parse rate per system and prompt variant; CTRL agreement_type distribution;\\n- exclusion and disjointness log (hash collisions found: expected 0 after exclusion);\\n- cost ledger per phase; licences; overlap between generator families and screen-judge families;\\n- the explicit warning 'HELD-OUT: iteration 2 must not tune any threshold on these rows'.\\n7c. README.md: layout, how to rerun (uv run pipeline steps 0-7, each resumable from raw/*.jsonl), a 'Restoring removed files' section, and the absolute workspace paths of data_out.json and screen_adjudicated_labels.json. Write .aii/manifest.yaml inside the existing .aii/ dir: keep data_out*, raw/, labeller/, dataset_card.md, prereg_strata.json, screen_adjudicated_labels.json, cost_ledger.jsonl; delete .venv/ (regenerable: uv sync) and any hf_cache/ (redownloadable: the HF ids above).\\n\\nFAILURE AND FALLBACK RULES:\\n- Budget overrun projected: shrink in this order: frontier subset → zero-shot to 1 family → CTRL panel to uncertain + 20% → screen-audit random sample 20% → 10%. Never cut the L25/L20/EXC panel coverage.\\n- A stratum turns out untestable (e.g. EXC has <50 CORRECT because models fail on exceptions): declare it untestable in prereg_strata.json. Do NOT add sentences after the labels are seen, unless the addition is decided by a rule written BEFORE the labels are looked at. That rule: if after Step 3 the tier-A-projected ERROR or CORRECT count of L25 is below 60, generate one more sha1-ordered batch of 100 L25 sentences with the 5 cheapest slots, budget permitting.\\n- Panel fails the gate across all reserves: ship auto labels only, with tier 'auto_only', and say so prominently. Iteration 2 then confirms only on tier A.\\n- tasksource/folio or folio-refined matching yields <150 eligible CTRL premises: take all eligible premises, and top up with MALLS-train short items (<20 words) as CTRL_MALLS, with the gold audit applied the same way.\\n- Logic-LM raw URLs 404: use the GitHub API contents endpoint. If that also fails, build track H only and flag track L as not rebuilt.\",", "+  \"dataset_search_plan\": \"No discovery is needed: every source is fixed, and most are already on disk. Work in /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_u75jRHUss0zo/3_invention_loop/iter_1/gen_plan/gen_plan_dataset_1/ (the executor's own workspace equivalent). Follow aii-python (uv, loguru), aii-parallel-computing (asyncio + Semaphore for OpenRouter, ProcessPool with spawn for z3), aii-long-running-tasks (pilot → scale) and aii-openrouter-llms. Track costs from the usage field of every OpenRouter response (request usage accounting) and append each call to cost_ledger.jsonl. Abort any phase whose running spend reaches its sub-cap, and stop everything at $9.5 cumulative.\\n\\n=== STEP 0. SETUP AND THE SHARED LABELLER (0:00-0:35) ===\\n0a. Copy these read-only files into labeller/: /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_u75jRHUss0zo/iter_3/gen_hypo/claude_agent/probes/{fol.py, repair_census.py, lint_smells.py, complexity_counts.py}. Copy these into data_local/: data/{yuan-yang__MALLS-v0__MALLS-v0.1-train.json, yuan-yang__MALLS-v0__MALLS-v0.1-test.json, yfxiao__folio-refined__train.csv, folio_refined_validation.csv, DSAVlab-UNIUD_FOLIO_validation-curated__FOLIO_instances.jsonl, DSAVlab-UNIUD_MALLS_test_subset-CURATED__MALLS_instances.jsonl}. If the iter_3 dir is missing, re-download: HF yuan-yang/MALLS-v0 (MALLS-v0.1-train.json / -test.json), yfxiao/folio-refined, DSAVlab-UNIUD FOLIO/MALLS curated sets (search the HF org DSAVlab-UNIUD).\\n0b. FIX ⊕ PRECEDENCE in fol.py. The grammar currently reads '↔ / ⊕ < → < ∨ < ∧'. Move ⊕ to the ∨ level, so it binds tighter than →. Regression tests (tests/test_fol.py): parse('A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)') == ('imp', ('xor', A, B), C); parse('A(x) ↔ B(x) → C(x)') keeps ↔ lowest; plus 10 random FOLIO formulas round-trip. SANITY: re-run repair_census.main() on the 99 curated pairs and confirm the COMPOUND count drops from 36 (the review found 3 were pure ⊕/→ bracketing), logging old vs new counts. If the count changes in an unexpected direction, stop and inspect before continuing.\\n0c. Write labeller/label_lib.py, which wraps repair_census.work() into two functions. equivalent_modulo_vocab(cand, ref) returns 'EQ' (plain z3 equivalence via fol.equivalent), 'VOCAB' (after align), 'GRAN' (after gran_bridge), 'NONEQ' or 'UNKNOWN' (z3 timeout). minimal_typed_repair(cand, ref, budget_s) returns (ops | None, secs, status in {FOUND, EXHAUSTED, TIMEOUT}). Keep the ALGORITHM identical to repair_census.py (same aligner thresholds and operators) so the screen and held-out labels come from one labeller. Reduce search budget_s from 25 to 8 s for runtime, and record 'COMPOUND_TIMEOUT' vs 'COMPOUND_EXHAUSTED' separately. Also add convention_flags(cand, ref), a DIAGNOSTIC ONLY field that does not change auto_label: SORTAL (differs only by an added unary restrictor from {Person, Human, People, Thing, Entity, Object, Individual}), XOR_OR (⊕ vs ∨ only), ARITY_REIFY, CONST_VS_EXISTS. Label map: EQ→CORRECT; VOCAB/GRAN→VOCAB_GRAN; NONEQ + FOUND→ERROR(ops); NONEQ + EXHAUSTED/TIMEOUT→COMPOUND; parse failure→UNPARSEABLE; UNKNOWN→TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN. Put z3 timeouts in every call (equivalent ms=3000), and label UNKNOWN rather than retrying forever.\\n0d. Input normaliser for LLM outputs, applied uniformly and logged per row as normalisation_applied. It strips code fences and a 'FOL:' prefix; maps ASCII to Unicode (forall/all x. → ∀x, exists → ∃, ->/=> → →, <-> → ↔, & → ∧, | → ∨, ~/!/not → ¬, xor → ⊕); and keeps the first formula line only. It also unwraps LaTeX, because the sibling run found phi-4 emits it: \\\\\\\\text{..}, \\\\\\\\( \\\\\\\\), $..$, \\\\\\\\forall, \\\\\\\\exists, \\\\\\\\land or \\\\\\\\wedge, \\\\\\\\lor or \\\\\\\\vee, \\\\\\\\neg or \\\\\\\\lnot, \\\\\\\\rightarrow or \\\\\\\\to, \\\\\\\\leftrightarrow, \\\\\\\\oplus. No other repair. Also check fol.py's variable-vs-constant rule: the sibling run found tokens like yr2028/p1080 read as variables and fixed the rule to ^[a-z][0-9]{0,2}'*$. Add a test and apply the same fix if this fol.py has the bug.\\n0e. Model discovery: GET https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/models and save it to models_snapshot.json. IDs change, so DO NOT hard-code them. Resolve each slot below by regex on id and family, taking the cheapest current instruct (non-thinking) endpoint, and write generation_manifest.json (slot, id, prompt/completion price, context).\\n  GENERATOR slots (9 families; none may be Anthropic, xAI, Zhipu, Moonshot or MiniMax, which are panel and reserve families): G1 Meta Llama small (llama-3.1-8b-instruct class) and G1b Llama large (llama-3.3-70b class; same family, reported as a size contrast); G2 Qwen3 (a 30B-A3B or 235B-A22B instruct, non-thinking); G3 Mistral (mistral-small-3.x); G4 DeepSeek (deepseek-chat V3.x, non-reasoning); G5 Google Gemma-3 (gemma-3-27b-it); G6 Microsoft Phi-4; G7 OpenAI small (gpt-4.1-mini, or gpt-5-mini with reasoning effort minimal); G8 Google Gemini Flash (2.5-flash or newer, reasoning disabled); G9 one more family for robustness (Amazon Nova Lite/Micro, or Cohere Command-R7B, whichever is cheapest). FRONTIER slot F: an OpenAI or Google frontier model (gpt-5-class with effort low, or gemini-pro-class), on the 200-item subset only. Gemini-Flash and Qwen are also likely screen-judge families. Record that overlap in the card; it matters for iteration 2's self-preference check, but it does not change the labels.\\n  PANEL slots (family-disjoint from all generators): P1 Anthropic claude-haiku-4.5 class. In the sibling run qY2a, Sonnet-4.5 FAILED the same kind of gate (0.775; it rejected contrapositives), so do not default to Sonnet. P2 xAI: the cheapest current Grok. grok-4-fast was gone in the sibling run, and its replacement grok-4.3 cost about $4.5 of $9.22 there. So run it with reasoning disabled and max_tokens 700. If the 30-sentence pilot projects P2 above $1.0 for Step 5, replace it with the reserve family. P3 Zhipu GLM (glm-4.6 or newer), run NON-thinking. RESERVE for a failing or too-expensive panel model: Moonshot Kimi-K2 class, then MiniMax. PANEL CACHE: key every panel response by (sentence_id, model_id, prompt_sha1, sorted class_ids), and never change keys for unchanged items. The sibling run re-billed everything after a key change.\\n\\n=== STEP 1. SENTENCE SELECTION (0:35-1:05), deterministic, seed 0, sha1 ordering ===\\n1a. Screen exclusion set: normalised-text hashes, where norm = lowercase, curly quotes to straight, strip everything except [a-z0-9 ], collapse whitespace. Cover all premises and conclusions of tasksource/folio validation (download via aii-hf-datasets), local folio_refined_validation.csv, the curated FOLIO and MALLS jsonl, MALLS-v0.1-test, and the Logic-LM FOLIO_dev contexts (step 6a). Log the size of the exclusion set.\\n1b. MALLS-train pool: 27,284 items. Parse gold with the fixed fol.py and drop unparseable gold (expect ≈334; log it). Dedupe on norm text. Drop excluded hashes. Compute words = len(text.split()); n_conditions (complexity_counts.nconds on gold); n_quant; depth; text_conditions = count of \\\\b(if|when|whenever|who|that|which|whose|unless|provided|only if|as long as|in case)\\\\b; exception_type. exception_type priority: unless > except > excluding > other_than > without > but_not. 'but not' counts only if the text does NOT match 'but not both' or 'either .* but not'.\\n1c. Stratum assignment, disjoint, in priority order. EXC = 100: all parseable items with unless/except/excluding/other than (≈72 expected), filled with 'without' then non-XOR 'but not' items with ≥15 words, sha1-ordered. L25 = 200 items with ≥25 words and n_conditions ≥3, sampled evenly over word bins 25-29 / 30-34 / ≥35 (take the shortfall from neighbouring bins). L20 = 150 items with 20-24 words and n_conditions ≥3. Supply check against complexity_counts.out.txt: 1,501 L25 and 4,539 ≥20-word items exist, so shortfalls should not occur; log them if they do.\\n1d. CTRL = 150 FOLIO-train premises. Download tasksource/folio train (premises-FOL is newline-separated). Match stories to local yfxiao__folio-refined__train.csv by the normalised premise list, then pair premises line by line; skip stories whose line counts differ. For each pair (orig_fol, refined_fol), set agreement_type in {IDENTICAL_STRING, EQ, VOCAB, GRAN, NONEQ, UNPARSEABLE} using equivalent_modulo_vocab. The eligible pool is EQ/VOCAB/GRAN/IDENTICAL_STRING. Prefer non-identical agreement: an identical string is only weak evidence that the refiner endorsed it, not independent annotation. Record agreement_type, and set reference = the refined formula. Stratify 50 items under 12 words, 60 at 12-19 and 40 at ≥20 words (take all ≥20-word items if fewer exist). Before sampling, reserve 6 FEW-SHOT EXEMPLARS from this pool and exclude them from CTRL. They must cover a universal conditional, a relative-clause restrictor, either-or (⊕), a negation, a named individual and a ≥2-condition conditional.\\n1e. Write sentences.json (600 rows with sentence_id = sha1('heldout|'+norm)[:12]) and prereg_strata.json: strata definitions, counts, exclusion counts and the testability rule, timestamped and frozen here.\\n\\n=== STEP 2. CANDIDATE GENERATION (1:05-1:50; sub-cap $3.5) ===\\n2a. Prompt (prompts/fewshot_v1.txt; save its sha1). The system message states the syntax: ∀ ∃ ¬ ∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕, CamelCase predicates with explicit arguments, lowercase constants, one formula, output exactly 'FOL: <formula>'. It asks for no ontology or vocabulary, which matches the user's ungrounded setting, and says nothing about how to render 'unless' (no convention hint). Then the 6 exemplars, then the sentence. The zero-shot variant (prompts/zeroshot_v1.txt) is the same text without exemplars. Settings: temperature 0, max_tokens 600, reasoning disabled or minimal where supported, 1 retry on transport error only. Do not re-sample when the output does not parse: an unparseable output IS the data.\\n2b. PILOT: 10 sentences × every slot. Measure the parse rate and the actual $/call from usage. Extrapolate the cost to the full sweep and log it. If the projection is above $3.5, first cut the frontier subset to 120, then cut zero-shot to 1 family. Drop any slot with parse rate <30% on the pilot and replace it with the reserve generator.\\n2c. Full sweep, asyncio with Semaphore(16), and resumable: responses are keyed by (sentence_id, slot, prompt_variant) and appended to raw/generations.jsonl. The runs: 600 × 10 slots (G1, G1b, G2-G9) few-shot, 600 × 2 zero-shot (G1b, G2), and 200 × F. The frontier subset is 60 L25 + 50 L20 + 40 EXC + 50 CTRL, sha1-ordered. Expected ≈7,400 calls, ≈$2.5-3.3.\\n2d. ccg2lambda. Load HF kenken6696/folio_by_ccg2lambda (train 1,640 / validation 365; columns original, logical_form; NLTK-style event semantics such as 'exists x.', '_larry(x1)', Subj/Acc roles). Use ONLY the train split and match on norm(original) to the CTRL sentences. Convert each match with nltk.sem.logic.Expression.fromstring, then emit fol.py syntax (strip the leading '_' and CamelCase the names; event variables stay). Label with system='ccg2lambda' and system_class='symbolic_eventsem'. Expect the aligner to fail on almost all of them (neo-Davidsonian events), so their labels come from the panel (tier B). Report the overlap count. If it is below 30 sentences, still include them but note that they are not analysable per system.\\n2e. Add the MALLS GPT-4 gold itself as system='malls_gpt4_gold' on the 450 MALLS sentences. It is a real 2023 GPT-4 output, and its label comes from the blind gold audit (label_source='panel_only'). Mark it so that iteration 2 can include or exclude it.\\n\\n=== STEP 3. AUTO-LABEL PASS 1 AND CLASS COLLAPSE (1:50-2:40, runs in the background while Step 4 runs) ===\\n3a. Parse and normalise every candidate. Per sentence, union-find all parseable formulas (candidates + reference) into EQUIVALENCE CLASSES modulo vocabulary: compare each new formula with the existing class representatives using equivalent_modulo_vocab; an UNKNOWN result opens its own class. Use a ProcessPool with 4 workers and a wall-clock guard. Scale 20 sentences → 100 → all, and extrapolate time at each step. Expected: at most 60 checks per sentence, about 20-30 min total.\\n3b. For every class not equivalent to the reference, run minimal_typed_repair(rep, reference) with budget_s=8. Labels propagate from the class representative to all member rows; store class_id and class_size. If the projected repair time exceeds 75 min, lower budget_s to 5 and log it (COMPOUND_TIMEOUT rises; the panel covers these).\\n\\n=== STEP 4. PANEL CALIBRATION GATE (overlaps Step 3; sub-cap $0.3 + $0.4 for the track-H real-error check) ===\\n4a. SYNTHETIC KNOWN-LABEL SET, 80 items on 20 sentences. Sentences: the 20 CTRL-pool premises NOT used in CTRL or exemplars, TRUSTED_AGREED, 10 of them with ≥15 words. Per sentence, build 4 variants (shuffled, sha1 seed): 2 UNFAITHFUL typed perturbations of the reference and 2 FAITHFUL variants. Perturbations: NEG, REV, QUANT, DROP of a condition, ADD of an atom from another sentence, SWAP; balanced 20 at DOWN positions (antecedent/restrictor) and 20 at UP positions, with the position computed by z3 monotonicity (fol.profile). Every perturbation must be z3-verified NON-equivalent. Faithful variants: 20 STRICT rewrites (conjunct reorder, De Morgan, contrapositive, prenex, variable rename), each z3-verified equivalent, and 20 synonym renames of predicate names (known VOCAB). Record the variant type.\\n4b. Run each panel model (plus Sonnet-class as a P1 alternative) in EXACTLY the production format (Step 5 prompt, disguised, batched per sentence). Gate per model: balanced accuracy ≥0.80 AND recall ≥0.70 on the faithful class AND ≥0.70 on the unfaithful class; report the DOWN vs UP split. A model below the gate is replaced by a reserve-family model, which is re-gated. If fewer than 3 models pass, run with 2 and mark every 1-1 split CONTESTED.\\n4c. REAL-ERROR CHECK against human experts: run the same panel on the curated track-H pairs (Step 6c; the 99 non-equivalent original→corrected pairs), with both formulas shown blind per sentence. Human verdict: corrected = faithful, original = unfaithful, excluding ambiguity-flagged items (READING_CHOICE, which are reported separately). Report per-model and majority accuracy on the unambiguous pairs, split by auto census class (single-op / two-op / COMPOUND). This is the panel's accuracy on REAL errors, the number the synthetic gate cannot give. It must be reported next to every panel-decided label rate. Contamination caveat: the corrected gold has been public since 2026-06; the disguise mitigates this.\\n\\n=== STEP 5. BLIND PANEL PASS: GOLD AUDIT + ADJUDICATION IN ONE (2:40-3:30; sub-cap $3.0) ===\\n5a. Disguise (labeller/disguise.py). Build a per-sentence lexicon: content-word stems of the text (spaCy en_core_web_sm lemmas + Porter) ∪ CamelCase/underscore tokens of every predicate and constant across ALL formulas of that sentence. Map each stem to a pronounceable CVCVC nonce, seeded by sha1(sentence_id), rejected if in WordNet, bijective. Rewrite the text token-wise, and rename predicate and constant names token-wise; logic, function, quantifier and number words stay unchanged. Guards: every disguised formula must parse; no two distinct symbols may collapse to one name (append a suffix if they would); on 50 sentences, assert that the pairwise equivalence structure is unchanged after disguise. The disguised text and formulas become row fields.\\n5b. ONE call per sentence per panel model. Shown: the disguised sentence and a shuffled list of the distinct disguised class representatives, with the reference class hidden among them, unlabelled, and UNPARSEABLE outputs not shown. Cap at 8 classes per call and split into 2 calls above that. Instruction: for each formula, decide whether it is faithful to the sentence, i.e. it says what the sentence says, no more and no less; different predicate names, decomposition, or logically equivalent form are fine. If unfaithful, give ALL applicable census operators from {NEG, REV, QUANT, RESTR, CONN, MOVE, DROP, ADD, SWAP, BIND, SCOPE, UNGLUE, OTHER}, a confidence 1-5, and flag whether the sentence is AMBIGUOUS (two legitimate readings). Output strict JSON; on a JSON failure, one re-ask. Settings: temperature 0, reasoning off or low, and reasoning tokens counted in cost.\\n5c. Cost estimate before launch: ≈600 calls × 3 models at about 1.1k input / 0.3k output tokens, i.e. about $0.004 per sentence for the three models together, ≈$2.4. Run 30 sentences first and re-extrapolate. If the projection is above $3.0, adjudicate CTRL only for classes auto-labelled VOCAB_GRAN/COMPOUND/TIMEOUT plus a 20% sentence sample (the direction's original scheme), and keep full coverage for the 450 MALLS sentences.\\n5d. GOLD AUDIT from the same votes, per sentence, using the reference class's votes. ≥2/3 faithful → GOLD_PANEL_OK (MALLS) or TRUSTED_AGREED (CTRL). ≥2/3 unfaithful → GOLD_WRONG (MALLS) or DISPUTED_REFERENCE (CTRL; the row moves to tier C).\\n\\n=== STEP 5e. REFERENCE REPAIR FOR GOLD_WRONG (sub-cap $0.6) ===\\nEach panel model writes its own FOL for the ORIGINAL sentence with the Step 2 few-shot prompt. If ≥2 of the 3 are mutually equivalent_modulo_vocab (EQ/VOCAB/GRAN), the reference becomes the one with the smallest symbol count among the agreeing formulas, status PANEL_REPAIRED. Otherwise the status is NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE. Then re-run Step 3b auto-labelling for those sentences against the new reference; the panel votes on candidate classes are reference-free and stay valid. Rows for NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE sentences get auto_label='NO_REF' and a panel-only label (tier C).\\n\\n=== STEP 5f. FINAL LABEL RULE (write it in the card verbatim) ===\\n- auto CORRECT (plain z3 EQ to the reference) → final CORRECT, tier A. Panel dissent is logged as panel false-alarm evidence.\\n- auto ERROR(ops) → final ERROR(ops), tier A, unless ≥2/3 panel models say faithful → CONTESTED (a likely convention, aligner or reference issue; convention_flags are recorded).\\n- auto VOCAB_GRAN → panel majority: faithful → CORRECT (correct_not_equivalent=true, tier B); unfaithful → ERROR('MEANING_RENAME' + panel ops, tier B).\\n- auto COMPOUND or TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN → panel majority: unfaithful → ERROR('COMPOUND', panel ops, tier B); faithful → CORRECT (correct_not_equivalent=true, tier B).\\n- If the panel majority flags the sentence AMBIGUOUS, add reading_choice=true and keep it out of primary pooling, as a separate class.\\n- A missing vote (a model failed) with a 1-1 split → CONTESTED.\\n- UNPARSEABLE → final UNPARSEABLE, kept in the denominator.\\n- NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE or DISPUTED_REFERENCE → final = panel majority, tier C (secondary only).\\nPrimary analyses use tiers A+B, exclude CONTESTED, and report sensitivity with CONTESTED counted as CORRECT and as ERROR.\\n\\n=== STEP 6. SCREEN AUDIT (3:30-4:15; sub-cap $1.3) ===\\n6a. Rebuild the screen exactly as the strategy defines it. Fetch the Logic-LM outputs from https://raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json and parse the 'FOL ::: NL' lines of raw_logic_programs. TRACK L: a (system, sentence, FOL) triple whose norm(sentence) matches a curated FOLIO-validation conclusion (reference = corrected gold) or a FOLIO-validation premise where original gold (tasksource validation) ≡ folio_refined_validation modulo vocabulary (reference = the refined formula). Match-rate fallback: if fewer than 150 conclusions match, add the matched stories' agreeing premises, as in the strategy. TRACK H: the 302 curated items, candidate = original gold, reference = corrected gold; ambiguity-flagged items → READING_CHOICE. item_id = sha1(system+'|'+normtext+'|'+candidate_fol)[:16], with candidate_fol the RAW string as it appears in the source. Also store (system, raw_text, normtext, raw_fol) so that iteration 2 can re-join if another experiment normalised differently. Report the match rate.\\n6b. Auto-label with the same labeller. Panel scope (sentence-level, direction's scheme): every sentence having any VOCAB_GRAN/COMPOUND/TIMEOUT class, plus a 20% sha1 random sample of the remaining sentences, plus all 99 track-H non-equivalent pairs (already run in 4c; reuse them, do not re-call). Same disguise, prompt and combination rule. Emit screen_adjudicated_labels.json as {item_id: {track, system, auto_label, repair_ops, panel_votes, final_label, label_tier, reading_choice, join_keys}}. Rows not sampled get final_label = auto_label and label_tier = 'auto_only'.\\n\\n=== STEP 7. ASSEMBLE, VALIDATE, DOCUMENT (4:15-5:30) ===\\n7a. data_out.json with 4 datasets. FIRST read the aii-json exp_sel_data_out schema. If input/output must be strings, input = json.dumps({text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, system, prompt_variant}, ensure_ascii=False) and output = final_label, with every other field mirrored as metadata_* keys: metadata_fold ('heldout' | 'screen_audit' | 'calibration'), auto_label, repair_ops, repair_status, panel_votes (per model: faithful, ops, conf, ambiguous), label_tier, label_source, correct_not_equivalent, reading_choice, gold_audit_flag, reference_status, convention_flags, strata {words, n_quant, depth, n_conditions, text_conditions, exception_type, source_stratum}, disguised_text, disguised_fol, class_id, class_size, sentence_id, item_id, normalisation_applied, generator_model_id, cost_usd. heldout item_id = sha1(system|normtext|raw_fol)[:16] (same recipe as the screen). Validate with aii-json, make mini and preview variants, and split files with aii-file-size-limit if needed.\\n7b. dataset_card.md, reporting DATA-QUALITY statistics only, no metric results:\\n- ERROR and CORRECT counts, rows and distinct sentences, per stratum (L25, L20, EXC by exception_type, CTRL by length bin) × tier; the testability declaration per stratum, copied into prereg_strata.json with a timestamp;\\n- gold-error rate of MALLS-train items per stratum (GOLD_WRONG / audited) with a Wilson 95% CI, and PANEL_REPAIRED vs NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE counts;\\n- correct-but-not-equivalent rate (final CORRECT among rows not plain-EQ to the reference), per stratum and per system;\\n- the automatic labeller's precision and recall vs the panel majority (binary auto CORRECT/VOCAB_GRAN vs ERROR/COMPOUND, on all panel-covered classes), plus the confusion matrix by auto class;\\n- panel calibration table (synthetic gate per model, DOWN/UP, STRICT/rename), real-error accuracy on track H, Fleiss κ per stratum, CONTESTED rate;\\n- parse rate per system and prompt variant; CTRL agreement_type distribution;\\n- exclusion and disjointness log (hash collisions found: expected 0 after exclusion);\\n- cost ledger per phase; licences; overlap between generator families and screen-judge families;\\n- the explicit warning 'HELD-OUT: iteration 2 must not tune any threshold on these rows'.\\n7c. README.md: layout, how to rerun (uv run pipeline steps 0-7, each resumable from raw/*.jsonl), a 'Restoring removed files' section, and the absolute workspace paths of data_out.json and screen_adjudicated_labels.json. Write .aii/manifest.yaml inside the existing .aii/ dir: keep data_out*, raw/, labeller/, dataset_card.md, prereg_strata.json, screen_adjudicated_labels.json, cost_ledger.jsonl; delete .venv/ (regenerable: uv sync) and any hf_cache/ (redownloadable: the HF ids above).\\n\\nFAILURE AND FALLBACK RULES:\\n- Budget overrun projected: shrink in this order: frontier subset → zero-shot to 1 family → CTRL panel to uncertain + 20% → screen-audit random sample 20% → 10%. Never cut the L25/L20/EXC panel coverage.\\n- A stratum turns out untestable (e.g. EXC has <50 CORRECT because models fail on exceptions): declare it untestable in prereg_strata.json. Do NOT add sentences after the labels are seen, unless the addition is decided by a rule written BEFORE the labels are looked at. That rule: if after Step 3 the tier-A-projected ERROR or CORRECT count of L25 is below 60, generate one more sha1-ordered batch of 100 L25 sentences with the 5 cheapest slots, budget permitting.\\n- Panel fails the gate across all reserves: ship auto labels only, with tier 'auto_only', and say so prominently. Iteration 2 then confirms only on tier A.\\n- tasksource/folio or folio-refined matching yields <150 eligible CTRL premises: take all eligible premises, and top up with MALLS-train short items (<20 words) as CTRL_MALLS, with the gold audit applied the same way.\\n- Logic-LM raw URLs 404: use the GitHub API contents endpoint. If that also fails, build track H only and flag track L as not rebuilt.\",", "   \"domain_practice\": \"What I read: the neurosymbolic handbook (critical rule: never score on FOLIO/MALLS as shipped, since about 39%/36% of the gold is wrong per 2606.02837; typecheck is not faithfulness); the strategy's field reasoning; the HF cards of tasksource/folio (1000/203; premises-FOL, conclusion-FOL) and kenken6696/folio_by_ccg2lambda (1,640/365, NLTK-style event semantics, CC-BY-4.0); the Logic-LLM repo listing (FOLIO_dev files for 3 systems confirmed); and this run's iter-3 probes and review audit. Norms from the meta-evaluation literature the strategy cites (TRUE; FRANK; Goyal & Durrett; Deutsch et al. 'Ties Matter') and LLM-jury practice (Verga et al. 2024, 'Replacing Judges with Juries'), recalled rather than re-fetched.\\n\\nHOW META-EVALUATION DATASETS OF THIS KIND ARE BUILT.\\n(1) CANDIDATES are REAL outputs of several systems, not perturbations. FRANK annotated outputs of 9 summarisers; TRUE standardised 11 datasets of real model outputs to binary item labels. Synthetic perturbations appear only as a secondary, per-type sensitivity diagnostic.\\n(2) LABELS are item-level and binary (consistent vs not). An error-type layer is optional but valued (FRANK's typology). Meta-evaluation labels come from multiple annotators with agreement reported (FRANK: 3 annotators, Krippendorff's α), plus adjudication or exclusion of low-agreement items. For formal-language outputs (semantic parsing, text-to-SQL), correctness is decided by EXECUTION or EQUIVALENCE checking against a reference (e.g. test-suite accuracy for SQL) rather than string match. Here z3 equivalence modulo vocabulary is the analogue, with the known failure of rejecting correct-but-non-equivalent outputs, which must be quantified.\\n(3) THE REFERENCE ITSELF IS AUDITED. 2606.02837 showed the FOLIO/MALLS gold is badly wrong, so any label derived from shipped gold must state its correction pipeline. Corrected gold exists only for FOLIO-validation (275) and the first 100 MALLS-test items, all of which the screen already uses, so a held-out set must audit its own gold.\\n(4) LLM-AS-ANNOTATOR PRACTICE. A panel of smaller models from disjoint families beats a single large judge and reduces self-preference bias (PoLL). A model's annotation is trusted only after its accuracy is measured on a known-label subset. The panel must not share a family with the systems whose outputs it judges. Contamination on public benchmarks is controlled by disguising (renaming entities or predicates).\\n(5) SIZE AND POWER. Item-level AUROC with about 300 positives and 300 negatives gives SE ≈ 0.02. Per-stratum claims need about 50+50 at minimum (MDE ≈ 0.10). Sub-strata below that are reported as untestable, not as results. Items are clustered by sentence (several systems per sentence), so distinct-sentence counts must be reported alongside row counts, and downstream bootstraps must cluster by sentence.\\n(6) DOCUMENTATION. Datasheets-style card: provenance and licence per source, selection criteria, label procedure and agreement, known biases (gold-error rate, correct-but-not-equivalent rate), exclusion and deduplication against the evaluation screen, and a held-out-use statement. Unparseable outputs remain in the denominator.\",", "   \"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS:\\n(a) Real outputs from 9 LLM families (plus a size contrast, a zero-shot variant, a frontier model, ccg2lambda and the 2023 GPT-4 gold as an extra real system). Synthetic perturbations are used ONLY for the panel calibration gate, never as candidate rows.\\n(b) Binary item labels with an error-type layer (repair ops from the solver, plus panel ops).\\n(c) Equivalence-based correctness against an AUDITED reference, with the correct-but-not-equivalent rate quantified as a first-class statistic.\\n(d) Multi-annotator adjudication with a measured known-label accuracy gate, Fleiss κ, and a CONTESTED class instead of forced labels.\\n(e) Family-disjoint panel (Anthropic/xAI/Zhipu vs 9 generator families) and disguised items against contamination.\\n(f) Power declared in advance: testability rule ≥50/50 rows plus ≥25 distinct sentences per side, frozen in prereg_strata.json before any metric runs; row and distinct-sentence counts both reported.\\n(g) Unparseable outputs kept; screen disjointness enforced by hash and logged.\\n(h) Datasheet-style card with licences and bias statistics.\\n\\nDEPARTURES, WHY, AND WHAT EACH COSTS:\\n(0) Evidence on record from the sibling run qY2a, whose dataset executor ran a similar 3-model panel. Panel-vs-human agreement on MALLS was 78.8% (κ=0.57). The panel judged 45.6% of MALLS gold and 62% of non-trivial FOLIO-train gold wrong. 43.9% of solver-non-equivalent candidates were panel-faithful. So expect large tier-B and DISPUTED_REFERENCE shares, and expect tier-B labels to carry roughly 20% noise. This is why tier A is kept separately and why 4c measures real-error accuracy. (1) LLM panel instead of human annotators. There are no humans in this pipeline and the budget is $10. Mitigations: the synthetic gate, AND a REAL-error accuracy check against the 2606.02837 expert corrections (track H, 4c), which is the only human-grounded accuracy number available. Cost: panel-decided labels (tier B: VOCAB_GRAN and COMPOUND, concentrated in the long stratum) carry the panel's error rate, which may be worse on long COMPOUND items than on the gate's short perturbations. The card must print tier-A-only counts next to A+B, so iteration 2 can confirm on tier A alone as a robustness check.\\n(2) The panel adjudicates ALL distinct classes, not uncertain items plus 20%. This departs from the direction but is a superset of it. Batching per sentence makes it cost about the same, gives the labeller's precision and recall on every class rather than on a sample, and folds the gold audit in blindly (the gold is hidden among the candidates, which removes the 'is this gold correct?' framing bias). Cost: batched comparative judgement can create contrast effects. This is mitigated by running the calibration gate in the identical batched format and shuffling the order per model.\\n(3) The MALLS reference is GPT-4 gold audited by LLMs, not expert-corrected. Expert-corrected gold does not exist outside the screen. Cost: residual reference errors that 2/3 panel models miss would flip labels. The gold-error rate with CI and the PANEL_REPAIRED / NO_TRUSTED_REFERENCE split are reported, and NO_REF items are confined to tier C.\\n(4) The CTRL 'two independent annotations' are partly NOT independent (folio-refined edits FOLIO; identical strings only signal non-correction). agreement_type is recorded, non-identical agreement is preferred, and the blind panel audit also runs on CTRL references.\\n(5) One prompt per generator (plus zero-shot for 2), temperature 0. There is no prompt-variance study; system-level Kendall τ downstream is therefore descriptive only. This is stated in the card.\\n(6) The repair search budget is cut from 25 s to 8 s for runtime, so COMPOUND partly means search timeout. COMPOUND_TIMEOUT vs COMPOUND_EXHAUSTED are recorded, and every COMPOUND goes to the panel anyway.\\n(7) ccg2lambda event-semantics outputs cannot be aligned by the vocabulary aligner, so they are panel-only labelled (tier B), a system-class confound. They are reported as a separate system class and never pooled into the per-LLM-family false-alarm claims.\\n(8) Condition counts come from the (noisy) gold formula, for comparability with the iter-3 supply counts. A text-derived count is stored alongside, so strata can be re-cut downstream without new labels.\",", "   \"builds_on\": \"This plan continues the iter-3 line. description: run_u75jRHUss0zo iter1 GEN_PLAN dataset_dir5 — held-out 600-sentence NL→FOL set (MALLS-train L25/L20/EXC + FOLIO-train CTRL); 9 LLM families plus ccg2lambda; one blind panel pass per sentence that also audits the gold; screen-audit labels keyed by item_id

GEN_PLAN written 2026-09-23 for dataset direction E of [[project_nl2fol_faithfulness_genstrat_iter1_run_u75jRHUss0zo]]. It reuses the iter_3 probes: fol.py (⊕ moved to the ∨ level), repair_census.py (wrapped as label_lib) and complexity_counts.py.

**Sentence strata**
- L25: 200 items. L20: 150 items.
- EXC: 100 items, with XOR 'but not both' excluded.
- CTRL: 150 FOLIO-train premises where original ≡ folio-refined. The plan records agreement_type, because identical strings are not independent evidence.

**Generators**
- 10 slots across 9 families (Llama 8B and 70B are one family), plus zero-shot runs for 2 families.
- A frontier model on 200 items.
- ccg2lambda: event semantics in NLTK syntax, labelled by the panel only.
- MALLS GPT-4 gold, included as a system.

**Panel**
- Haiku-4.5 + Grok + GLM. Sonnet failed the gate in the sibling run [[project-nl2fol-faithfulness-dataset-exec-iter1-run-qy2a2is-wlis]], and Grok was the cost hog there.
- Every distinct equivalence class is adjudicated in a batched per-sentence call. The gold is hidden among the candidates, so the gold audit is blind.
- Gate: 80 synthetic items plus a real-error check on the track-H human-corrected pairs.

**Labels**
- Tiers: A (auto, solver-backed), B (panel-decided), C (no trusted reference).
- An auto ERROR that ≥2/3 of the panel call faithful becomes CONTESTED.
- Budget sub-caps: generation 3.5, gate 0.7, panel 3.0, reference repair 0.6, screen 1.3; hard stop at $9.5.
```

### [67] ASSISTANT · 2026-09-23 13:02:17 UTC

```
The plan for the held-out confirmation set is written to `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json` in the workspace. I did not run a JSON parser over it; I only checked by eye that the backslash escapes are valid.

**What the executor will build**
- **600 sentences, none of them in the screen.** Overlap is excluded by hashing the normalised text against FOLIO-validation, MALLS-test and the Logic-LM contexts. The groups are:
  - 200 MALLS-train sentences of 25+ words with 3+ conditions;
  - 150 of 20–24 words with 3+ conditions;
  - 100 with real exception markers. "Either … but not both" is left out, because it means exclusive-or rather than an exception.
  - 150 FOLIO-train premises where the original annotation and the folio-refined one agree.
- **Candidate translations:** about 7.4k from 9 model families (few-shot, plus zero-shot for 2 families), a frontier model on 200 items, ccg2lambda where sentences overlap, and the original MALLS GPT-4 gold treated as one more system.
- **Automatic labels** come from the same labeller the screen uses (`fol.py` and `repair_census.py` from the earlier round), after two fixes:
  - **⊕ precedence:** ⊕ now binds tighter than →. As a regression check, re-running the old census should drop the COMPOUND count below 36.
  - **Input clean-up:** a normaliser for LaTeX output. The parallel run's dataset executor found phi-4 writes formulas in LaTeX.
- **Three-model review panel (Anthropic / xAI / Zhipu classes).** It sees disguised items and makes one batched call per sentence covering every distinct candidate. The dataset's own reference formula is hidden among the candidates, so the check of that reference is blind.
- **Panel qualification.** Each panel model must first pass two checks:
  - 80 items where the right answer is known;
  - the 2606.02837 expert-corrected real errors from the screen's track H. This is the only accuracy number grounded in human judgement.
- **Final labels** are sorted into three tiers: A (solver-backed), B (decided by the panel) and C (no trusted reference). Items where the panel and the solver disagree are marked CONTESTED rather than forced.
- **Screen audit:** the screen's track-L and track-H items are rebuilt with the same item_ids. Their adjudicated labels go to `screen_adjudicated_labels.json`, with the raw join keys stored in case another experiment normalised text differently.
- **Budget:** each phase has its own spending cap, the whole run stops at $9.5, and there is a fixed order for cutting scope if costs run over.

**Two departures from the direction:**
- **The panel reviews every candidate group, not just the uncertain ones plus a 20% sample.** Batching one call per sentence makes this cost about the same, and it gives the labeller's precision and recall on everything rather than on a sample.
- **The FOLIO "two independent annotations" are partly not independent.** Folio-refined is an edit of FOLIO, so an identical formula only shows the refiner left it alone. The plan records how each pair agrees and sends those references through the blind panel check as well.

**What to expect from the labels:** a parallel run (qY2a) built a similar panel. Its panel agreed with humans only 78.8% of the time (κ = 0.57), and it judged 45.6% of MALLS gold wrong. So expect a large share of panel-decided labels with roughly 20% noise, and plan for iteration 2 to confirm on the solver-backed tier A alone as well.

Compute profile is `cpu_plus`, target is 4 datasets, and I saved a memory note for this plan.
